COMMON JUDGMENT : (per Justice G. Sri Devi)
The lis in this batch of Appeals preferred by the claimants
pertain to common acquisition proceedings and therefore, they
are heard together and being disposed of by this common
judgment
2. Heard iearned counsel for the claimants and Sri D.Kiran,
learned Assistant Government Pleader for the respondent.
Perused the material available on record.
3. The present acquisition was for the purpose of submergence
of priyadarshini Joorala Project/ancillary projects under
Priyadarshini Joorala Project. This Court uittejudgment, dated
L5.O9.2O22 has already elaborately analysed the evidence and
consid.ered the identical issue raised in these appeals in
L.A.A.S.No.4 of 2OL6. Both the counsel have not disprrted tl.e
factum of commonatity of the evidence and the issue in these
appeals and in L.A.A.S.No.4 of 2OL6, wherein this court has
allowed the appeal enhancing the market value by two more

2
times (1+21 than the value fixed by the L.A.O. with all other
consequential benefits as stipulated under the amended Act.
4. Therefore, following the judgment passed by this Court in
ir
L.A.A.S.No.4 of 2016, flated 15.09.2022 and for the reasons
{
stated therein, all these appeals stand disposed of by enhancing
the market value of the acquired land by two more times (1+2)
].
than the value fixed by the L.A.O. with all other consequential
benefits as stipulated under the amended Act, No order as to
costs
Miscellaneous petitions, if any, shall stand closed-JUSTICE G. SRI DEVI
JUSTICE M.G. PRTYAI'ARSINI

Appeal filed under Section 54 of the L.A.Act, aggrieved by the Order and
decree dated 03.03.2010 passed in O.P.No.151 o 2009 on the file of the Court of
the Senior Civil Juctge at Wanaparthy.
This appeal coming on for hearing and upon perusing the grounds of
appeal, the Judgment and Decree of the Court below and the material papers in
the case and upon hearing the arguments of Sri. V. Manohar Rao, Advocate for
the appellant and GP for Appeals for the Respondent.
This Court doth Order and Decree as follows:
1. That the appeal be and herby is allowed ;
2. That the Market value is enhanced by two more times (1+2) than the value
fixed by the Land Acquisition ffioer, with all consequertial benefits;
3. That appellant be and hereby is not entitled to interest for the delay period
of 5437 days in preferring the appeal.
4. That there shall be no order

period of 5440 days in prefening the appeal.

Appeal filed under section 54 of the L.A.Act, aggrieved by the order and
decree dated 03.03.2010 passed in O.P.No.127 of 2OO9 on the file of the Court of
the Senior Civil Judge at Wanaparthy.
This appeal coming on for hearing and upon perusing the grounds of
appeal, the Judgment and Decree of the Court below and the material papers in
the case and upon hearing the arguments of Sri. V. Manohar Rao, Advocate for
the appellant and GP for Appeals for the Respondent.
This Court doth Order and Decree as follows:
1. That the appeal be and herby is allowed ;
2. That the Market value is enhanced by two more times (1+2) than the
value fixed by the Land Acquisition officer, with all consequential
benefits;
3. That appellant be and hereby is not entitled to interest .for the delay
period of 5433 days in prefering the appeal.
4. That there shall be no order as to

Appeal filed under Section 54 of the L.A.Act, aggrieved by the Order and
decree dated 03.03.2010 passed in O.P. No. 217 of 2f,og on the file of the Court of
the Senior Civil Judge at Wanaparthy.
This appeal coming on for hearing and upon perusing the grounds of
appeal, the Judgment and Decree of the Court below and the material papers in
the case and upon hearing the arguments of Sri. V. Manohar Rao, Advocate for
the appellant and GP for Appeals for the Respondent.

benefits;

period of 5334 days in preferring the appeal.

Appeal filed under Section 54 of the L.A.Act, aggrieved by the Order and
decree dated 03.03.2010 passed in O.P.No.189 of 2009 on the file of the Court of

This appeal coming on for hea!'ing and upon perusing the grounds of

benefits;

period of 5431 days in preferring the appeal..
